# Colab experiment handoff

Base commit `e061a06bcee023294764ae24a3bb30266d197edd`. The ver2 supplement restores `261d3263f2754a1cfd4dc6a8d84e175ff8edbadb`. All archive members are relative to the repository root except the Git bundle.

## Fresh-checkout results

Verified locally after extraction into a separate checkout at e061a06. No model was retrained.

| Check | Result |
|---|---|
| pipeline/provenance/verify.py runs/provenance_v1 | Passed reconstruction and source/order checks for 42 graphs and 540,288 nodes. |
| pipeline/provenance/verify_rtl.py | Passed for 42 tensors and 1,078 chunks. |
| pipeline/provenance/verify_rtl.py --expansion | Passed for ten tensors and 826 chunks. |
| pipeline/verify_artifact.py | Passed for the legacy pilot, 42 samples and three independent clean source groups. Its scope excludes the stepwise tables. |
| experiments/stepwise_rebuild/run.py --stage report | Failed during calibration. The first missing input was `runs/stepwise/rebuilt_gnn/predictions.json`. The original stepwise run tree, including model states and per-node outputs, was unavailable. |
| Three LOFO protocol inventories | All 80, 94 and 100 graph records matched their protocol hashes. |
| ver2 restore | The bundle was verified and fetched from the base checkout to the exact requested commit. RTL sources and all 219 mapped JSON exports matched the two triplet manifests. |

The committed stepwise fusion table reports 21/21 clean and 20/21 infected correct, yielding 97.62%. Recomputing from its aggregate counts confirms the arithmetic. The stepwise per-sample predictions needed for an independent reproduction are missing. Rescoring the earlier pilot predictions supplied here with the commit’s metrics gives 42/42 (100%). External fusion labels all ten samples infected, giving 5/10 and 50% balanced accuracy. The earlier pilot’s external AUROC is 0.84 and the stepwise table reports 0.78. Only the thresholded external result agrees. Neither the full table nor bit-identical Colab training is verified.

The October 31 supplied graph ZIP, the earlier trusthub/features export, and the no-ABC provenance rebuild have different node identities and synthesis flows. The legacy embeddings include testbenches. Use corrected testbench-free tensors for fusion. The original corrected development embedding manifest omitted the model revision. Four tensors were spot-checked historically against the later pin. PIC source includes an undriven c_in bit. The b15 comparison shows structural churn and has no released inserted-cell label file. The audit flags s15850-T100 and s35932-T300 for correspondence discrepancies and quarantines s38584-T300 for a trigger/payload disconnect. A frozen candidate file is still pending review.

The archives are local handoff files. Automatic approval review rejected the GitHub push because the destination was not verified. The ver2-history ZIP supplies the exact commit offline on top of e061a06.

## Restore on Colab

Put this directory in Google Drive and mount Drive in Colab. In a Colab Bash cell, set PACK to the mounted handoff directory. Run the following in a new checkout. Extract the archives after checkout to preserve recorded source bytes and mixed line endings. Allow roughly 6 GB of free space plus the Python environment.

```bash
set -eu
git clone https://github.com/sahersh17/HT_detection_GNN.git /content/ht
cd /content/ht
git checkout --detach e061a06bcee023294764ae24a3bb30266d197edd
(cd "$PACK" && sed -nE 's/^- `([a-f0-9]{64})  ([^`]+\.zip)`$/\1  \2/p' PACKAGE_MANIFEST.md | sha256sum -c -)
for z in "$PACK"/*.zip; do
  case "$(basename "$z")" in ver2-*) continue ;; esac
  unzip -oq "$z"
done
python -m pip install -r experiments/stepwise_rebuild/requirements-lock.txt
python pipeline/provenance/verify.py runs/provenance_v1
python pipeline/provenance/verify_rtl.py
python pipeline/provenance/verify_rtl.py --expansion
python pipeline/verify_artifact.py
```

For a new training run, use `python experiments/stepwise_rebuild/run.py --stage compare` followed by `--stage external` for the diagnostic. The full historical report also needs the outputs from gnn, hosts, build/audit and calibration. See the runner for stage order. Colab installation and training were not tested. To restore ver2 separately, continue from the base checkout:

```bash
unzip -op "$PACK/ver2-history-01.zip" ver2.bundle > /tmp/ver2.bundle
git fetch /tmp/ver2.bundle refs/heads/ver2:refs/heads/handoff-ver2
git worktree add /content/ht-ver2 handoff-ver2
cd /content/ht-ver2
for z in "$PACK"/ver2-*.zip; do
  case "$(basename "$z")" in ver2-history*) continue ;; esac
  unzip -oq "$z"
done
unzip -oq "$PACK/legacy-embeddings.zip"
```

The review coordinator should give reviewers only review-packet-01.zip. The separate key, checkout, candidate files and protocols are for the coordinator.

## Cohorts and inputs

The LOFO experiments use graphs. Each protocol's samples array gives the family, benchmark, side, exact graph path and SHA-256. All listed graph files matched after unpacking.

- `data/lofo_detection/protocol.json` — 80 graphs. AES, MC8051, PIC16F84, RS232, b15, memctrl, s38584
- `data/lofo_expanded/protocol.json` — 94 graphs. AES, MC8051, PIC16F84, RS232, b15, memctrl, s15850, s35932, s38417, s38584
- `data/lofo_expanded_b19/protocol.json` — 100 graphs. AES, MC8051, PIC16F84, RS232, b15, b19, memctrl, s15850, s35932, s38417, s38584

The paired AES/RS232 folds are in `data/integrated/paired_manifest.json` and `group_folds.json` in the same directory. Corrected CodeBERT manifests are `runs/rtl_clean_v1/manifest.json` and `runs/rtl_expansion_v1/manifest.json`. MC8051 and Trojan-only BasicRSA embeddings are in `runs/new_rtl_v1`, and b19 embeddings are in `runs/b19_rtl_v1`, each with its own manifest. The five mapped-netlist families b15, s15850, s35932, s38417 and s38584 have no corresponding original RTL embeddings in these protocols. The separate RS232 mapped-netlist archive must not be conflated with the four paired RS232 RTL benchmarks. GHDL-derived source spans can point to generated Verilog rather than original VHDL.

## Inference and references

The evaluated detector scores the suspect graph, with the same suspect design’s RTL chunks for fusion. Clean designs provide training examples and paired evaluation controls. No same-host golden circuit enters the forward pass. Suspect-only inference is the defensible deployment assumption. TrojanLoC classifies module and line embeddings from the submitted RTL. LoRD exploits distinctive gate-level subgraph signatures in contest netlists. Neither paper validates transfer on these hosts. [TrojanLoC](https://arxiv.org/abs/2512.00591) · [LoRD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.17922).

If a trusted reference is explicitly supplied, evaluate it as a separate paired task. All 11 LOFO families have paired clean examples, with repeated clean designs. BasicRSA has only infected examples here. A paired example alone does not establish trustworthy gate correspondence. For AES, diff the paired RTL and intersect changed lines with Yosys source ranges on the suspect graph. For s38584, s35932, s38417 and s15850, the mapped-netlist audit uses preserved instance names and compares cell type, parameters and port connections. Added cells are candidates, changed shared cells remain unknown. RS232 source variants and the other paired families do not have validated gate-by-gate truth. After independent synthesis, use fixed tools and options, source spans, hierarchy and connectivity anchors, and check ambiguous cones with equivalence tools. Leave split, merged and ambiguous gates unmatched. Never transfer a row index between graphs. The graph ID and ordered-node hash identify rows within a single export. Zero independently reviewed gate labels are supplied.

For unseen hosts keep the published graph threshold of 0.5 to reproduce the fixed protocol. To choose a deployment threshold, hold out entire host families in an outer evaluation. Within training families only, choose a single threshold using family-held-out predictions and a prespecified criterion such as family-macro balanced accuracy or a false-positive budget. Freeze it before scoring the outer host. Report per-family sensitivity and false positives. The historical training-only fusion threshold of 0.233915 still scored 50% externally. Node localization needs its own reviewed validation labels or a declared ranking budget.

## ver2 modality coverage

The precision cohort contains 67 new verified hosts and six earlier anchors. For all 219 manifest rows the supplied RTL and mapped Yosys JSON matched their recorded hashes. These are mapped netlists from which graphs are derived. The admission report excludes the Trojan variant of tt_um_synth_simple_mm from graph probes because synthesis left no usable gates. The 72 hosts below have usable graph and RTL inputs on all three arms. The cohort remained on hold with zero admitted training records.
